Method: 提出ZeroSplat框架：无需训练且无需预存语义特征，通过稳健的多视图几何约束将2D视觉语言模型（VLM）先验提升至3D空间，从而实现内在的点级理解。

Result: 在GR-LERF和GR-ScanNet两个新基准上，ZeroSplat在泛化场景和单目标场景下均显著优于现有最先进方法，同时保持卓越的效率。

Conclusion: ZeroSplat通过无训练、零特征的设计有效解决了现有R3DGS方法的瓶颈，在广义指代分割任务上实现了性能与效率的双重提升。

Abstract: Recent advancements in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled language-guided scene understanding. However, existing Referring 3D Gaussian Splatting (R3DGS) methods are fundamentally restricted to single-target queries. To reflect the ambiguity of real-world instructions, we introduce the Generalized Referring 3D Gaussian Splatting Segmentation (GR3DGS) task, which requires dynamically segmenting an arbitrary number of targets (0, 1, or $N$). To facilitate comprehensive evaluation of this new task, we construct two new benchmarks: GR-LERF and GR-ScanNet. Crucially, existing R3DGS paradigms exhibit fundamental technical bottlenecks that severely limit their performance on the GR3DGS task: they lack intrinsic 3D point-level understanding by operating merely on 2D rendered pixels, and they incur prohibitive computational overhead by requiring per-scene optimization to embed heavy semantic features. To dismantle these bottlenecks, we propose ZeroSplat, a novel training-free and zero-feature framework. ZeroSplat lifts 2D Vision-Language Model (VLM) priors into 3D space through robust multi-view geometric constraints. This strategy enables intrinsic point-level understanding without incurring any additional feature storage. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ZeroSplat significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods across generalized and single-target scenarios while maintaining exceptional efficiency. Project Page: https://inkmind-ai.github.io/ZeroSplat

</details>


### [37] [In-Context Learning for Wound Classification with Small Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18819)
*George Martvel,Oskar Gustafsson,John Pavia,Ernst Ahlberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了小型多模态语言模型（SMLMs）通过检索增强的上下文学习（ICL）实现无需训练的伤口图像分类。实验在Kaggle（1469张，10类）和Medetec（560张，9类）数据集上评估了11个模型。结果表明，基于查询条件的ICL显著优于零样本和随机少样本提示，最佳模型Qwen 3.5 27B在Kaggle上达到0.872准确率，在Medetec上达到0.678准确率。检索增强的ICL能够在无需任务特定重训练的情况下进行适应性分类，且支持紧凑的上下文部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统伤口图像分类依赖大量人工标注数据和任务特定的监督学习，当标签空间或部署环境变化时需要重新训练，缺乏灵活性和泛化能力。本研究旨在探索小型多模态语言模型能否通过检索增强的上下文学习提供一种无需训练的替代方案。

Method: 在Kaggle伤口数据集（1469张，10类）和Medetec数据集（560张，9类）上，评估了Qwen 3.5、Ministral 3和Gemma 4三大系列的11个小型多模态语言模型。采用零样本提示、随机少样本提示、基于嵌入的k近邻（kNN）检索、以及kNN检索后接最大边际相关性重排序（MMR）等多种策略，并设置了加权kNN控制实验、支持集缩减实验和上下文大小扫描来评估检索效果、模型规模和提示长度的影响。

Result: 查询条件化的上下文学习一致优于零样本和随机少样本提示。在Kaggle数据集上，Qwen 3.5 27B使用kNN+MMR达到最佳效果：准确率0.872，F1分数0.871。在Medetec数据集上，同一模型达到准确率0.678，F1分数0.670。大模型表现优于匹配的加权kNN控制实验，表明模型不仅使用最近邻投票，还能有效利用检索到的示例。检索增强的ICL在支持集缩减时性能下降有限，且大多数收益在8-10个支持图像时饱和。

Conclusion: 检索增强的上下文学习使小型多模态语言模型能够在不进行任务特定重训练的情况下实现适应性伤口图像分类。紧凑的检索上下文有助于实际部署和隐私保护，但性能仍依赖于模型规模、检索策略和数据集难度。

Abstract: Wound image classification is often treated as a task-specific supervised learning problem, requiring substantial amounts of manually labelled data and retraining when the label space or deployment setting changes. This study evaluated whether small multimodal language models (SMLMs) can provide a training-free alternative for wound classification through retrieval-based in-context learning (ICL). Experiments used two public wound-image datasets: the Kaggle wound dataset (1469 images, 10 classes) and the Medetec dataset (560 images, 9 classes). Eleven SMLMs from the Qwen 3.5, Ministral 3, and Gemma 4 families were evaluated under zero-shot prompting and few-shot prompting with random support examples, embedding-based k-nearest-neighbour (kNN) retrieval, and kNN retrieval followed by maximal marginal relevance reranking (MMR). Retrieval-only weighted-kNN controls, support-set reduction experiments, and support-context size sweeps were used to assess the effects of retrieval, model scale, and prompt length. Query-conditioned ICL consistently outperformed zero-shot and random few-shot prompting. On the Kaggle dataset, the best result was achieved by Qwen 3.5 27B with kNN+MMR, reaching 0.872 accuracy and 0.871 F1 score. On Medetec, Qwen 3.5 27B with kNN+MMR reached 0.678 accuracy and 0.670 F1. Larger models exceeded matched weighted-kNN controls, indicating use of retrieved examples beyond nearest-neighbour voting. Retrieval-based ICL degraded modestly under support-set reduction, and most gains saturated with 8-10 support images. Retrieval-based ICL allows SMLMs to perform adaptable wound image classification without task-specific retraining. Compact retrieved contexts may support practical and privacy-conscious deployment, although performance remains dependent on model scale, retrieval strategy, and dataset difficulty.

</details>


### [38] [Open-Vocabulary Gaze Object Prediction: Benchmark and Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18827)
*Binglu Wang,Sensen Niu,Ying Chen,Guangyu Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiSG基准和开放词汇注视目标预测（OVGOP）框架，通过文本驱动目标发现和梯度引导参数选择，在开放和封闭词汇设置下均取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注视目标预测方法局限于封闭词汇和场景特定数据集，难以处理真实世界中长尾分布和未见类别的目标。

Method: 1）构建含86个野外类别的DiSG基准；2）文本驱动目标发现定位候选对象；3）注视引导选择模块锁定目标；4）GIST方法选择性更新与类别词汇最相关的参数。

Result: 在开放词汇设置下表现有效，在传统封闭词汇设置中优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的开放词汇框架和基准有效推动了注视目标预测在真实场景中的应用，代码已开源。

Abstract: Gaze Object Prediction (GOP) aims to localize and recognize the objects humans attend to, a task crucial for understanding human-centric interactions. However, existing methods are typically trained under a closed-vocabulary paradigm with a fixed label space and evaluated on scene-specific datasets, limiting their applicability to real-world scenarios where gaze targets often follow a long-tail distribution or belong to unseen categories. To address this gap, we introduce Diverse Scenes for Gaze object prediction (DiSG), a benchmark containing 86 in-the-wild categories that facilitates the evaluation of Open-Vocabulary GOP (OVGOP). Building on DiSG, we propose a framework that leverages text-driven object discovery to localize potential gaze candidates, with a gaze-guided selection module to pinpoint the intended target from the candidate objects. Furthermore, to better capture semantic knowledge across diverse in-the-wild categories, we introduce Gradient-Informed Selection Tuning (GIST) to selectively update parameters most relevant to a given class vocabulary. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed model performs effectively in open-vocabulary settings and also outperforms existing methods in the conventional closed-vocabulary setting. The benchmark and code is available at https://github.com/sensniu/ovgop.

</details>


### [39] [OPD-IAD: From Language Judgment to Industrial Anomaly Detection via On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18850)
*Shuimu Chen,Jing Jin,Nan Su,Hongbo Xu,Zebang Cheng,Wenming Yang,Fei Ma,Guijin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OPD-IAD框架，通过证据特权密集在线自蒸馏和语言引导的视觉锚定方法，在利用大视觉语言模型进行工业异常检测时，实现语言引导而非主导的像素级异常定位，在多个指标上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LVLM的工业异常检测方法能从语言判断中生成图像级异常判断和可解释的缺陷推理，但在生成精确的像素级异常图方面仍有困难，语言往往主导而非引导视觉响应。

Method: 提出OPD-IAD框架：(1) 证据特权密集在线自蒸馏，将特权缺陷证据蒸馏到模型自身的在线判断轨迹中，实现密集监督；(2) 语言引导的视觉锚定，通过判断重正向编码，将最终判断条件转化为语义锚点，与密集视觉特征进行对比生成异常图。

Result: 在大多数图像级、像素级和QA指标上，OPD-IAD在基于LVLM的IAD方法中取得了最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: 语言判断提供紧凑的语义引导，密集视觉特征仍是像素级评分的基础，实现了语言引导异常定位而不让语言质量直接决定像素级响应。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have recently shown strong potential for industrial anomaly detection (IAD) by providing image-level anomaly judgments and interpretable defect reasoning. However, current LVLM-based IAD methods still struggle to produce precise pixel-level anomaly maps from generated language judgments. We aim to achieve precise pixel-level localization while using language as guidance rather than letting it dominate the visual response. Specifically, we propose \textbf{OPD-IAD}, an evidence-privileged dense on-policy self-distillation framework for LVLM-based IAD. OPD-IAD distills privileged defect evidence onto the model's own on-policy judgment trajectory, enabling the final generated judgment to be learned under dense supervision rather than treated only as a textual answer. The resulting judgment serves as a semantic condition for dense anomaly perception. To turn this condition into dense visual evidence, we introduce \textbf{Language-guided Visual Anchoring}, which uses a judgment reforward to re-encode the image and question under the final-judgment condition into semantic anchors and contrasts them with dense visual features through a contrastive heatmap head to generate anomaly maps. The language judgment therefore provides compact semantic guidance, while dense visual features remain the basis for pixel-level scoring, allowing language to guide anomaly localization without letting language quality directly dictate the pixel-level response. Extensive experiments show that OPD-IAD achieves the best overall performance among LVLM-based IAD methods, leading on most image-level, pixel-level, and QA metrics.

</details>


### [40] [Think Sparse, Predict Dense: Continuous Thought Machines for Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18856)
*Zekai Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DQ-CTM，将连续思维机扩展到密集视觉预测任务（如图像超分辨率），通过窗口级紧凑表示和密集查询机制实现逐步重建，实验表明在固定4步训练下PSNR从28.10 dB提升至30.28 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将连续思维机的内部时间维度扩展到密集视觉预测任务（如图像超分辨率）面临挑战，因为空间证据需要在每个输出位置保持可用，而非压缩为单一全局表征。

Method: 提出DQ-CTM：在窗口级别使用CTM产生紧凑摘要表示，通过结构化低秩、参数高效的紧凑到密集查询机制，将紧凑思维表示转换为窗口对齐的密集查询；每个窗口内位置获得独立查询，共享思维动态逐步细化密集表示。具体实例ThinkSR将编码特征图划分为局部视觉窗口，经共享细化后恢复原始特征场并解码为高分辨率图像。

Result: 在固定4步训练下，PSNR-Y从28.10 dB提升至30.28 dB，PSNR-RGB从26.63 dB提升至28.78 dB，平均ℓ1误差从0.0346降至0.0235。所有100张评估图像从T=1到T=4均有所改善。

Conclusion: 初步实验验证了稀疏潜在思维用于密集空间重建的可行性，为密集视觉任务中更广泛的连续思维架构提供了动机。

Abstract: Continuous Thought Machines introduce an internal temporal dimension in which neuron-level histories and synchronization-derived representations evolve over a sequence of thought ticks. Extending this mechanism to dense visual prediction is non-trivial, because tasks such as image super-resolution require spatial evidence to remain available at every output location rather than being compressed into a single global representation. In the proposed window-level use of CTM, the thought dynamics produce a compact summary representation for each local window. DQ-CTM transforms this compact thought representation into window-aligned dense queries through a structured low-rank, parameter-efficient compact-to-dense query mechanism. Each position within a window receives its own query, while shared thought dynamics progressively refine the dense representation across ticks. In its super-resolution instantiation, termed ThinkSR, encoded feature maps are partitioned into local visual windows without token pooling, restored to the original feature field after shared refinement, and decoded into a high-resolution image. Preliminary experiments under a fixed four-tick training horizon reveal a progressive reconstruction trajectory. PSNR-Y increases from 28.1045 dB at $T=0$ to 30.2817 dB at $T=4$, while PSNR-RGB increases from 26.6271 dB to 28.7781 dB and the mean $\ell_1$ error decreases from 0.034602 to 0.023545. All 100 evaluated images improve from $T=1$ to $T=4$. These initial results establish the feasibility of sparse latent thought for dense spatial reconstruction and motivate broader continuous-thought architectures for dense vision.

</details>


### [41] [Reliability-Aware 3D Geometric Injection for Universal Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18863)
*Bohan Su,Jiashuo Wang,Fangyi Liu,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniGeo提出一种通用单目3D增强行人重识别框架，通过一致性感知可靠性门控和双流残差融合，在统一模型中处理遮挡、换装和跨模态等复杂场景，在不破坏2D特征空间的前提下安全引入3D几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D表示因缺乏深度和拓扑感知而存在空间模糊性，而简单引入单目3D先验在极端视觉退化下会产生几何估计噪声导致负迁移，因此需要一种安全利用3D几何不变性和规范结构属性的方法。

Method: 将3D信息处理解耦为几何提取和动态利用两个阶段：首先将单目3D参数投影为运动学关节表示以捕捉实例级几何拓扑；然后通过一致性感知门控机制将3D先验作为后期结构残差隔离，自适应过滤几何噪声并实现向纯2D基线的可控回退。

Result: 在多种复杂场景（遮挡、换装、跨模态）下显著提升性能，同时在干净域上保持有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 通过解耦3D信息处理并设计残差融合与门控机制，UniGeo成功实现了安全、鲁棒的单目3D增强通用行人重识别，代码已开源。

Abstract: Universal person re-identification (ReID) aims to retrieve pedestrian identities across diverse real-world scenarios, including severe occlusions, clothing changes, and cross-modality shifts, within a unified model. However, existing 2D representations fundamentally struggle with spatial ambiguities due to a lack of depth and topological awareness, while naively introducing monocular 3D priors often causes severe negative transfer due to geometric estimation noise under extreme visual degradation. To safely harness the clothing-invariant and canonical structural properties of 3D geometry, we propose UniGeo, a Universal Monocular 3D-Enhanced ReID framework driven by a Consistency-Aware Reliability Gate and Dual-Stream Residual Fusion. Specifically, the processing of 3D information is strategically decoupled into geometric extraction and dynamic utilization. To provide pure structural compensation, we project monocular 3D parameters into kinematic joint representations, explicitly capturing instance-level geometric topology to resolve appearance-based ambiguities. To robustly incorporate these cues without perturbing the reliable 2D feature space, we isolate the 3D prior as a late-stage structural residual; modulated by the consistency-aware gate, this mechanism adaptively filters geometric noise and enables controlled fallback to the pure 2D baseline. Extensive experiments show that our method improves challenging, structure-sensitive scenarios while preserving competitive performance on clean domains. Code is available at https://github.com/BohanSu/UniGeo.

</details>


### [42] [Wave2Body: Rethinking mmWave Human Pose Estimation as Radar-to-Body Token Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18875)
*Bo Liang,Chen Gong,Wei Gao,Chenren Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Wave2Body提出了一种雷达到人体骨架的Token翻译框架，通过自监督毫米波Token化器、预训练组合身体Token化器和轻量级翻译器，实现从稀疏毫米波雷达点云中恢复完整3D人体姿态，在跨域泛化上优于现有方法且计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接回归关节点坐标，将雷达感知、人体结构和对齐耦合在一起，在模糊的雷达观测下容易学习数据集特定的捷径，且依赖有限的配对监督数据。

Method: 提出Wave2Body框架：1) 自监督毫米波Token化器学习雷达特征；2) 预训练的组合身体Token化器定义输出空间；3) 轻量级翻译器将雷达Token映射到身体Token。

Result: 在M4Human和mmBody数据集上，Wave2Body相比现有方法实现了更强的跨域泛化能力，同时训练和推理的计算成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 解耦雷达感知、人体结构和对齐这三个学习目标，通过Token翻译的方式能有效应对毫米波雷达点云稀疏、视角依赖带来的3D姿态恢复不适定问题。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar enables privacy-friendly human sensing, but its sparse point clouds are physical measurements of view-dependent electromagnetic reflections and only indirectly characterize body articulation. Recovering a complete 3D pose from such partial, geometry-dependent observations is therefore under-constrained. Existing methods directly regress joint coordinates from paired radar-pose data, relying on the same limited paired supervision to learn radar perception, human-body structure, and their alignment. This coupling can encourage dataset-specific shortcuts under ambiguous radar observations. We propose Wave2Body, a radar-to-body token translation framework that decouples these learning targets using a self-supervised mmWave tokenizer, a pretrained compositional body tokenizer that defines the output space, and a lightweight translator between them. Experiments on M4Human and mmBody show that Wave2Body achieves stronger cross-domain generalization than previous methods while incurring much lower computational costs for training and inference. All the code and experiment results are publicly available at https://github.com/Galaxywalk/Wave2Body.

</details>


### [43] [Local Label-Informed Feature Transfer for Generating Ground-Truth Medical Images: A Comparison of GAN- and Diffusion-Based Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18882)
*Rick Wilming,Irem Ozseker,Luca Matteo Cornils,Ahcène Boubekki,Benedict Clark,Danny Panknin,Stefan Haufe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LLIFT框架，通过GAN和扩散模型两种方式生成带有真实病灶的半合成脑部MRI图像，无需像素级标注，为可解释AI方法提供带空间控制的地面真值数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI验证方法依赖专家标注（易出错）或人工扰动（缺乏临床真实性），亟需一种能生成具有真实病灶且可控制位置的医学图像验证数据集。

Method: 提出LLIFT框架，包含两种生成范式：LLIFT-GAN（仅从二分类标签学习病理特征的自定义GAN）和LLIFT-DM（基于ControlNet的扩散模型修复流程，以边界框掩码为条件）。

Result: 两种方法在脑部MRI数据上均取得了与真实病理分布可比的FID分数，定性检查确认病灶结构真实。

Conclusion: LLIFT可生成具有空间可控地面真值的基准数据集，用于评估医学影像中的XAI方法。

Abstract: Validating Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) methods in medical imaging requires ground-truth data with known locations of informative features. However, current approaches rely on expert annotations, which are prone to labeling errors, or on hand-crafted artificial perturbations superimposed onto healthy images to mimic lesions or malignant features, which lack clinical realism. We present Local Label-Informed Feature Transfer (LLIFT), a framework for generating semi-synthetic brain magnetic resonance images with realistic lesions placed in user-controlled regions, which does not require pixel-level lesion annotations during training. We implement LLIFT with two generative paradigms: LLIFT-GAN, a custom GAN that learns pathological features from binary class labels alone, and LLIFT-DM, a diffusion-based inpainting pipeline conditioned on bounding-box masks via ControlNet. Both approaches are evaluated on brain magnetic resonance imaging data derived from the Human Connectome Project. In evaluations, both achieve Fréchet Inception Distance scores, with respect to the real pathological distribution, that are comparable to the inter-class reference between healthy and pathological images in the given dataset. Furthermore, qualitative inspection confirms the realism of lesion structures. The resulting benchmark datasets provide spatially controlled ground truth data for evaluating XAI methods in medical imaging.

</details>


### [44] [SynGallery: A Synthetic Gallery of Real Paintings for Instance-Level Artwork Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18907)
*Patryk Bartkowiak,Jakub Markil,Bartosz Kotrys,Dominik Michels,Sören Pirk,Wojtek Palubicki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SynGallery合成数据集，通过3D画廊场景渲染生成多视角训练数据，在不收集真实照片的情况下提升艺术品检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实例级艺术品识别中，训练集（目录照片）与测试集（游客拍摄）之间存在显著的视角、光照、反射等场景差异，现有方法缺乏弥补这一鸿沟的训练数据。

Method: 将目录图像置于程序生成的3D画廊场景中，从多视角渲染，保留原始作品身份。生成24,490张渲染图，覆盖4,898幅画作。

Result: 仅用SynGallery训练，GAP从67.18提升至73.47；加入完整Met训练集后，GAP从35.97提升至38.48。

Conclusion: 合成数据可有效替代真实照片采集，且性能提升主要源于几何视角变化而非逼真度；模糊、传感器噪声等反而降低性能。

Abstract: Instance-level artwork recognition requires matching a handheld visitor photograph to a specific work in a large museum collection. This is challenging because painting datasets typically provide clean catalog images for training, while test queries are captured under oblique viewpoints, gallery lighting, reflections, frames, and other scene-level variations. We present SynGallery, a synthetic gallery dataset for artwork retrieval that addresses this gap without collecting additional real photographs. Starting from catalog images of real paintings, we place each artwork into a procedurally generated 3D gallery scene and render it from multiple viewpoints under varied geometric and appearance conditions, while preserving the exact identity of the original work. The resulting dataset contains 24,490 rendered views of 4,898 paintings from the Met benchmark. We show that these synthetic views provide a stronger training signal than the corresponding studio photographs. At the same number of training data points, training only on SynGallery improves art painting recognition from 67.18 to 73.47 GAP$^-$. When added to the full Met training set, SynGallery improves the published benchmark protocol from 35.97 to 38.48 GAP. Ablation experiments show that the gain comes primarily from geometric viewpoint variation rather than photographic realism: blur, sensor noise, and image compression consistently reduce performance.

</details>


### [45] [TAP-RAG: Task-Aware Policy Control for Long-Document Multimodal Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18917)
*Zhong Ji,Keqi Jin,Yan Zhang,Jiasheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAP-RAG是一个任务感知策略控制框架，通过预测任务先验、估计证据信号并执行自适应策略，显著提升了长文档多模态问答的准确性，在DocBench和MMLongBench-Doc上分别比基线提升9.1和4.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态RAG系统通常采用查询无关的证据使用策略，无法根据不同查询需求灵活调整证据行为，导致长文档多模态问答效果受限。

Method: 提出TAP-RAG框架，包含任务感知策略控制器（TAPC）预测任务先验和证据信号并生成可执行策略；策略引导的TA-QFD在文档图上扩展文本与结构证据；TAVE选择性检查页面图像以获取视觉/布局证据；最后通过带守卫的融合阶段合成多模态证据，并在证据不足时放弃回答。

Result: 在DocBench上准确率从61.1提升至70.2（+9.1），在MMLongBench-Doc上从42.2提升至46.7（+4.5），均达到最佳整体准确率。

Conclusion: 任务感知的灵活证据策略能有效提升长文档多模态问答性能，验证了针对不同查询需求动态调整证据使用方式的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Long-document multimodal question answering requires more than retrieving relevant chunks from a large document. Different queries require different evidence behavior. Existing multimodal RAG systems improve evidence access through text chunks, page images, graph links, or heterogeneous document elements, but they often apply a largely query-agnostic evidence-use strategy. We present TAP-RAG, a task-aware policy-controlled RAG framework for long-document multimodal QA. TAP-RAG contains a main controller, the Task-Aware Policy Controller (TAPC), and two policy-guided evidence executors: Task-Aware Query-Guided Flow Diffusion (TA-QFD) and Task-Aware Visual Enhancement (TAVE). For each query, TAPC predicts the task prior, estimates visual/local/global evidence signals, and produces an executable policy. TA-QFD then expands textual and structural evidence over the multimodal document graph, while TAVE selectively inspects page images when visual or layout evidence is needed. A guarded synthesis stage fuses text, visual, and structural evidence and abstains when support is insufficient. On DocBench and MMLongBench-Doc, TAP-RAG achieves the best overall accuracy among the compared systems, improving over a matched multimodal-RAG baseline by +9.1 points (61.1 to 70.2) and +4.5 points (42.2 to 46.7), respectively.

</details>


### [46] [Learning Explicit Physical Parameter Control and Benchmarking for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18924)
*Yanxun Li,Hao Wen,Bingze Song,Jiashu Zhu,Aiming Hao,Chubin Chen,Jintao Chen,Jiahong Wu,Xiangxiang Chu,Miao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PhyParam，一个基于物理参数引导的图像到视频生成模型，通过大规模物理参数化数据集和轻量级注意力路由机制，实现了对刚体运动（力、质量、摩擦、恢复系数、重力等）的精确控制，显著提升了视频生成的物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到视频生成模型虽然能生成看似合理的运动，但缺乏对明确物理因果关系的可靠控制，在多物体交互中实例级约束容易泄露或纠缠。作者认为根本原因在于缺乏大规模细粒度物理参数化数据以及能将物理属性正确绑定到实例并强调动态而非外观的模型设计。

Method: 1) 构建PhyParam-Dataset，包含13万段带密集物理参数的仿真视频（涵盖5种刚体运动类型）；2) 提出PhyParam模型，通过轻量级物理注意力路由机制（physical-attention routing）将物体级的力、质量、摩擦、恢复系数以及场景级重力作为条件注入扩散模型；3) 采用语义-结构特征空间监督（semantic-structural feature-space supervision）增强运动学习；4) 建立PhyParam-Bench基准，包含多层次物理一致性评估协议。

Result: 实验表明PhyParam在保持高视觉保真度的同时显著提升了物理一致性，实现了对显式刚体物理参数的可控图像到视频生成。

Conclusion: 这项工作为图像到视频生成中的物理参数化控制提供了有效方案，数据集、基准和代码将开源以支持未来研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in image-to-video generation have improved visual realism, making physically grounded and controllable dynamics an important step toward future world simulation.
  Current models often generate plausible motion, but it is not reliably governed by explicit physical causes, and instance-level constraints can leak or become entangled in multi-object interactions.
  We attribute this gap to two missing pieces: large-scale, fine-grained physical parameterization, and model designs that correctly bind physical attributes to instances and emphasize dynamics over appearance.
  To bridge this gap, we introduce PhyParam-Dataset, an interaction-centric collection of 130K physically simulated videos with dense physical parameterization, including force vectors, object material properties, and environmental constants across five representative rigid-body motion types.
  Built on this data, we present PhyParam, a physics-guided image-to-video diffusion model that conditions on object-level forces, masses, friction, restitution, and scene-level gravity via a lightweight physical-attention routing mechanism, and further improves motion learning with semantic-structural feature-space supervision.
  We also establish PhyParam-Bench, a benchmark for physical-law consistency in image-to-video generation, with a multi-level protocol evaluating temporal dynamics, spatial stability, and semantic--physical alignment. Experiments show that PhyParam improves physical consistency while maintaining high visual fidelity, advancing explicit rigid-body physical-parameter control for image-to-video generation.
  We will publicly release the dataset, benchmark, and code to support future research.

</details>


### [47] [Dual Adversarial Fine-tuning for Enhancing Robustness of Large Vision Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18958)
*Sibo Wang,Jie Zhang,Shiguang Shan,Xilin Chen,Wen Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双对抗微调框架，通过联合优化视觉和语义监督信号，在不修改模型架构或单独重训练的情况下，仅替换CLIP视觉编码器即可提升LVLMs在零样本分类、图像描述和VQA等多任务上的对抗鲁棒性，效果优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗防御方法主要针对单任务场景（如零样本分类），缺乏跨多模态任务的泛化能力，而LVLMs的视觉输入容易受到对抗攻击，存在重大安全隐患。

Method: 提出双对抗微调框架，包含两个核心分支：(1)视觉监督分支，利用冻结的原始视觉编码器提取干净图像特征来引导对抗鲁棒性；(2)语义监督分支，引入图像-文本对齐作为上下文信号以保持语义一致性。通过简单替换CLIP视觉编码器实现跨任务鲁棒性，无需任务特定的重新训练或架构修改。

Result: 在零样本分类、图像描述和VQA任务上的对抗鲁棒性评估中，该方法均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 双对抗微调框架有效提升了LVLMs在多任务场景下的对抗鲁棒性和泛化能力，为多模态安全防御提供了一种通用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: While Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), represented by LLaVA and GPT-4V, have demonstrated remarkable capabilities, their visual inputs remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks, posing significant security risks. Existing defense methods predominantly target single-task scenarios (e.g., zero-shot classification) and consequently lack generalizability across various multimodal tasks. To address this limitation, we propose a dual adversarial fine-tuning framework that jointly optimizes visual and semantic supervision signals from two modalities, enhancing model robustness while generalizing across multiple downstream tasks. The proposed framework comprises two core components, i.e., $\textbf{Visual}$ supervision branch and $\textbf{Semantic}$ supervision branch. The former branch leverages features from clean images, extracted via a frozen original vision encoder, to guide adversarial robustness while the latter incorporates caption-image alignment as a contextual signal to preserve semantic coherence under attack. Moreover, our method achieves cross-task robustness by simply replacing the CLIP vision encoder in the original model, with no need of separate task-specific retraining or architecture modifications.Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms the state-of-the-art method in adversarial robustness evaluation across zero-shot classification, image captioning, and visual question answering (VQA) tasks.

</details>


### [48] [DobicVLM: Aligning Chest X-Ray Report Generation with Clinically-Grounded Programmatic Rewards via Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18988)
*Thanni Adewuyi,Angelica Obayi,Andem Aniekan,Samuel Okoko,Angel Ezendu,Ephraim Usani,Ademide Animasaun,Philip Chibundu,Christian Maurice,Mary Donald Essien,Oluwaseun Odunsi,Oluwasegun Oguntuase,Abiodun Adereni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DobicVLM 结合 MedGemma-4B 监督微调与基于 GRPO 的临床规则奖励，在少量数据下提升了胸部 X 光报告生成的结构完整性、解剖准确性和语义忠实度，在盲审中多项指标超越 Gemini 2.5 Flash。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动胸部 X 光报告生成方法在结构遵循性、解剖完整性和语义忠实度方面存在不足，亟需一种无需神经奖励模型的透明对齐方法。

Method: 基于 MedGemma-4B 进行监督微调，结合 Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 和基于规则的可解释奖励组件（结构验证、解剖检查表、语义相似度、长度约束），在 1000 对去标识临床数据上训练。

Result: 在 69 例盲审中，DobicVLM 在印象准确率（27.2%）和医学术语使用（86.5%）上优于 Gemini 2.5 Flash 和 MedGemma 4B 基线，在完整性和转诊建议上略有不足。

Conclusion: GRPO 结合可编程规则奖励为资源受限场景下的透明医学报告对齐提供了有效方案，验证了强化学习在医学影像报告生成中的价值。

Abstract: Medical imaging is a cornerstone of diagnostics, yet automated chest X-ray report generation struggles with structural adherence, anatomical completeness, and semantic faithfulness. We introduce DobicVLM, a vision-language model combining supervised fine-tuning on MedGemma-4B with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and clinically-grounded programmatic rewards. Our approach uses interpretable, rule-based reward components; structural verification, anatomical checklist, semantic similarity, and length constraints to enforce clinical standards without neural reward models. Trained on 1,000 de-identified image-report pairs from a private clinical dataset (with ethics approval and compliance to local regulations), DobicVLM is evaluated via blinded expert review on 69 held-out cases. DobicVLM outperforms Gemini 2.5 Flash across the majority of criteria, achieving the highest impression accuracy (27.2%) and medical terminology (86.5%) compared to both Gemini 2.5 Flash and MedGemma 4B baselines, with minor trade-offs in completeness and referrals. This demonstrates GRPO's value for transparent alignment in resource-limited settings. Keywords: Vision-Language Models, Radiology Report Generation, Reinforcement Learning, Medical AI, GRPO

</details>


### [49] [SWITi: Quantifying and Reducing Tiling Artifacts with Sliding Window Inner Tiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18990)
*Federico Carrara,Aman Kukde,Melisande Croft,Joran Deschamps,Florian Jug*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SWITi是一种测试时方法，通过平均重叠滑动窗口预测来减少神经网络分块预测中的拼接伪影，特别适用于后验采样模型，且不增加额外前向传播成本。同时提出了两种无参考指标（FRT和ASV）用于检测和量化伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块预测在处理大图像时不可避免，但会产生拼接伪影，尤其是在后验采样模型中，伪影容易被误认为生物结构，影响下游分析。

Method: SWITi：平均重叠滑动窗口预测，将相邻样本间的差异分散到不同位置而非积累在固定接缝处；FRT和ASV指标：基于分块置换检验比较接缝处与周围像素梯度分布。

Result: 在三个荧光显微镜数据集（2D和3D）上，SWITi显著减弱了拼接接缝，同时提高了重建保真度和分辨率。

Conclusion: SWITi能有效去除或减少后验预测中的分块伪影，避免被误认为生物结构，对生物医学大图像数据处理具有重要意义。

Abstract: SWITi is a test-time method for reducing artifacts in tiled predictions, particularly for neural networks that learn posterior distributions from which solutions are sampled at inference time. Tiled predictions are unavoidable for large image data, and artifacts arise whenever tiles are smaller than a network's receptive field and when tiles are independent posterior samples. SWITi averages overlapping sliding-window predictions, so discrepancies between neighboring samples are spread across shifted tile positions rather than accumulating at fixed seam coordinates. For posterior models, SWITi uses no more tile samples than an MMSE estimate requires and therefore incurs no additional forward passes. Additionally, we introduce two reference-free metrics, the Fraction of Rejected Tests (FRT) and Artifact Severity (ASV), for detecting and quantifying tiling artifacts from a per-tile permutation test that compares the distribution of pixel gradients across tile seams against the surrounding image content. On pre-trained and published image splitting models across three fluorescence microscopy datasets in 2D and 3D, we show that SWITi substantially attenuates stitching seams while also improving reconstruction fidelity and resolution. Since tiling artifacts in posterior predictions can easily be mistaken for biological structures or for boundaries between biological structures, removing or reducing them using SWITi will improve the downstream processing of large image predictions, which is particularly relevant for biomedical data.

</details>


### [50] [Benchmarking Deep Learning Approaches for AEC Engineering Drawing Layout Detection and Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18997)
*Tianyang Huang,Alessio Lombardi,Ahmed Elnagar,Ahmed Zalouk,George Paul,Sepehr Najjarpour,Arvid Sigurdsson,Khalid Ismail,Mohamed Ragab,Edlira Vakaj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对AEC图纸信息提取中布局检测这一关键环节，构建了专用数据集并基准测试了五种深度学习架构。RF-DETR在mAP50上达到0.949，Qwen3-VL在F1-score上达到0.911，而通用文档预训练模型存在"领域干扰"导致性能下降，为AEC自动化IE奠定了技术基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AEC图纸信息提取长期受限于人工低效，而布局检测作为组织图文层次的关键"中间件"未被充分探索，通用文档布局模型缺乏在工程图纸上的验证。

Method: 构建AEC专用布局数据集，对五种深度学习架构进行基准测试，包括RF-DETR和视觉语言模型Qwen3-VL等。

Result: RF-DETR达到最优mAP50为0.949，Qwen3-VL取得领先F1-score为0.911；通用文档预训练模型出现"领域干扰"导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 为AEC领域自动化信息提取建立了稳健的技术基础，证明了专用模型相比通用模型在工程图纸上的优越性。

Abstract: Information Extraction (IE) from Architecture, Engineering, and Construction (AEC) drawings remains hindered by manual inefficiency, while Layout Detection, a vital 'middleware' organizing graphical and textual hierarchies, is underexplored. General document layout models, optimized for text-centric content, lack validation on engineering drawings. This study constructs a custom AEC-specific layouts dataset and benchmarks five deep learning architectures. RF-DETR achieves state-of-the-art performance with an $mAP_{50}$ of 0.949, while the Vision-Language Model Qwen3-VL attains a leading F1-score of 0.911. Conversely, models pre-trained on general document datasets suffer from "domain interference", causing performance degradation. This establishes a robust technical foundation for automated IE in AEC.

</details>


### [51] [Learning Semantic-Robust Change Detection via Semantic-Invariant Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19000)
*Jiuhe Qu,Yingping Liang,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCDistill框架，通过语义不变自蒸馏策略和扩散模型扰动模拟管道，学习鲁棒的语义变化检测，在多个基准上达到SOTA，并泛化到二值变化检测和变化描述任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变化检测模型的特征容易受光照、阴影、大气变化等非语义因素干扰，导致误检和泛化能力不足。

Method: 1）语义不变自蒸馏策略：从扰动后语义一致的数据中学习语义鲁棒性，提取抗干扰特征；2）基于扩散模型的扰动模拟管道：合成复杂环境变化，增强模型区分语义变化与外观波动的能力。

Result: 在多个语义变化检测基准上达到最先进性能，并展现出对二值变化检测和变化描述任务的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: SCDistill通过数据和表示双重层面的鲁棒性促进，实现协同性能提升，有效降低非语义扰动导致的误报。

Abstract: Change detection aims to identify semantic changes between remote sensing images. However, features from models are easily disturbed by non-semantic variations, such as illumination, shadows, and atmospheric changes, leading to false alarms and limited generalization in real-world scenarios. In this paper, we propose \textbf{SCDistill}, a framework for learning semantic-robust change detection via semantic-invariant self-distillation. First, to strengthen semantic consistency, we introduce a semantic-invariant self-distillation strategy that learns semantic robustness from perturbed yet semantically consistent data, empowering the change detector to extract disturbance-resistant features and achieve more reliable and accurate semantic change identification. Second, to expand paired data with non-semantic variations, we design a diffusion-based perturbation simulation pipeline that synthesizes complex environmental changes, enabling the model to explicitly learn to distinguish semantic changes from appearance-level fluctuations and reduce false alarms caused by non-semantic disturbances. These components promote robustness from data and representation perspectives, leading to synergistic performance gains. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SCDistill achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple semantic change detection benchmarks and exhibits strong generalization to binary change detection and change captioning tasks. Code is accessible at https://github.com/elecreak/SCDistill.

</details>


### [52] [Mitigating Modality and Language-Style Gaps for Zero-Shot Video Moment Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19027)
*Jihyun Lee,Cheol-Ho Cho,Woojin Jun,Woojin Jeong,Jae-Pil Heo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于自相似性的视频时刻检索方法Self-SiMS，通过利用视频内部自相似性而非查询-视频相似性来生成和评分时刻候选，有效克服模态和语言风格差异，达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本视频时刻检索方法过度依赖查询与视频内容的相似性，容易受到模态差异和语言风格差异的影响，导致候选片段不可靠和检索结果不稳定。

Method: 提出Self-SiMS方法，包含两个核心：1) 基于自相似性的时刻候选生成与评分，仅从视频内容中挖掘自相似性来生成鲁棒的片段候选；2) 查询感知的多模态大语言模型推理阶段，进一步对齐文本与视频。

Result: 在多个零样本视频时刻检索基准上达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过利用视频内部自相似性替代查询-视频相似性，可以有效缓解模态和语言风格差异问题，显著提升零样本视频时刻检索的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Zero-shot video moment retrieval aims to overcome the limitations of traditional approaches that require large-scale datasets annotated with text and its relevant temporal spans. Despite advances in pre-trained vision-language models and multimodal large language models, existing ZMR methods still heavily depend on query-to-video content similarity, making them vulnerable to modality and language-style gaps. These gaps lead to unreliable span proposals and unstable moment retrieval results. To address this issue, we propose Self-Similarity-based Moment Proposal and Scoring that instead exploits intrinsic relationships within videos, enabling robust span generation and scoring. By deriving self-similarity only from the video content, we circumvent the noisy and mismatched patterns of query-frame or query-caption similarities, thereby mitigating both modality and language-style gaps. Furthermore, we introduce a query-aware MLLM-based reasoning stage to further sharpen alignment between text and video. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Self-SiMS achieves state-of-the-art performance across ZMR benchmarks.

</details>


### [53] [IMMoE: Incomplete Multi-View Anomaly Detection via Mixture of View Experts Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19032)
*Lei Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对实际工业场景中多视角异常检测因视角信息缺失导致性能下降的问题，提出了不完整多视角异常检测（IMVAD）任务，并构建了RIMAD数据集和IMMoE方法，通过多视角专家融合与局部异常增强编码器有效处理缺失视角信息，在RIMAD和Real-IAD数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视角异常检测方法假设所有视角完全可用，但在实际工业场景中常因遮挡等问题导致视角信息缺失，使现有方法因缺乏多视角一致性先验而性能下降。

Method: 提出IMMoE方法，包含两个关键模块：(1) 多视角专家融合（MVEF）通过多视角专家网络有效融合多视角信息并指导单视角重建；(2) 局部异常增强编码器（LAEE）通过对局部特征施加dropout防止模型过拟合掩码区域。同时提出自动生成IMVAD数据集的流程，并基于Real-IAD构建了RIMAD数据集。

Result: 在RIMAD和Real-IAD数据集上均达到最优性能，在RIMAD上像素级指标提升11.8%，图像级指标提升2.8%。

Conclusion: 提出的IMVAD任务和IMMoE方法有效解决了实际工业场景中视角信息缺失下的异常检测问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Existing Multi-view Anomaly Detection (MAD) methods assume that all views are completely available and model each view separately. However, in real industrial scenarios, information in the view may be missing due to faults such as occlusion, which leads to the performance degradation of existing methods due to the lack of a multi-view consistency prior. To address this, we explored a more challenging task: Incomplete Multi-View Anomaly Detection (IMVAD), in which some areas of each view were masked. We proposed a pipeline for automatically generating the IMVAD dataset and generated the \textbf{RIMAD} dataset based on the Real-IAD dataset through this pipeline. In addition, in order to effectively utilize the information of multiple views in the absence of view information, we propose \textbf{IMMoE}, which consists of two key modules: (1) Multi-View Expert Fusion (MVEF) effectively fuses multi-view information through a multi-view expert network and guides the reconstruction of a single view; (2) Local Anomaly Enhancement Encoder (LAEE) effectively prevents the model from overfitting the mask region by applying dropout to local features. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on both the RIMAD and Real-IAD datasets, especially on RIMAD, we have increased the pixel-level and image-level metrics by 11.8\% and 2.8\%, respectively. Our source code is available at https://github.com/HULEI7/IMMoE

</details>


### [54] [CoGoal3D: Collaborative 3D Object Detection with 3D-Aware Fusion and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19036)
*Zhihao Yang,Zhiyu Xiang,Peng Xu,Tianyu Pu,Kai Wang,Eryun Liu,Dongping Zhang,Yong Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoGoal3D框架，通过两阶段流水线解决V2X协同3D检测中的空间错位问题，在三个数据集上实现SOTA性能，3D AP@0.7提升超10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X协同感知方法主要关注2D BEV检测，在3D检测任务中因忽略协同车辆间高度和姿态差异导致的3D空间错位，性能不佳。

Method: 提出两阶段流水线CoGoal3D：第一阶段设计多尺度3D感知全局融合模块缓解空间错位；第二阶段通过3D点重建辅助任务细化候选框；并引入多智能体协同数据增强策略。

Result: 在DAIR-V2X、V2V4Real和V2X-Real数据集上，3D AP@0.7分别提升10.86%、10.34%和10.18%，达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: CoGoal3D有效解决了协同3D检测中的空间错位问题，数据增强策略进一步提升了性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: V2X collaborative object detection features overcoming the limitations of single-vehicle systems by aggregating environmental features from multiple collaborative agents. However, existing mainstream V2X perception methods mainly focus on 2D BEV object detection. When 3D detection task is concerned, inferior results are obtained because they ignore the 3D spatial misalignment caused by differing height and attitude among the collaborators. In this paper, we propose a novel collaborative 3D object detection framework called CoGoal3D, which extracts and refines the 3D feature gradually in a two-stage pipeline. In the first stage, a multiscale 3D-aware global fusion module is designed to mitigate the 3D spatial misalignment. The resulting proposals are then refined in the second stage with an auxiliary task of 3D point reconstruction. An effective multi-agent collaborative data augmentation strategy is further proposed to enrich the training data while minimizing information loss. Extensive experiments on public real-world datasets demonstrate that our CoGoal3D achieves new state-of-the-art performance, with 3D AP@0.7 improvements of 10.86%, 10.34%, and 10.18% on the DAIR-V2X, V2V4Real, and V2X-Real datasets, respectively. Code is available at https://github.com/Megalo-f/CoGoal3D.

</details>


### [55] [FilmWorld: Agentic Novel-to-Film Generation through Dynamic Cinematic World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19038)
*Jialong Zuo,Haotong Zuo,Shiwei Zhang,Xiang Wang,Chen Li,Nong Sang,Changxin Gao,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FilmWorld系统，将小说到电影的生成形式化为动态电影世界建模，分为构建和演化两个阶段，通过多智能体协作实现长篇幅、多场景的视觉叙事生成，并引入FilmEval评估框架在15部小说上验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型仅擅长短片段、单场景内容，难以处理小说到电影这一需要长时长、多场景、跨镜头实体状态一致性的复杂任务。

Method: 将任务分解为构建（文学叙事结构化、实体状态建模、镜头规划）和演化（状态锚定视觉生成、跨镜头状态传播、闭环验证）两阶段，由两组专业智能体协作完成。

Result: 在15部代表小说的基准测试中，FilmWorld在叙事保真度和跨场景一致性方面显著优于现有最先进的视频生成智能体系统。

Conclusion: FilmWorld通过动态电影世界建模有效解决了长篇幅、多场景视频生成中的因果一致性和视觉连贯性问题，为AI驱动的长视频生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Translating novels into films poses a grand challenge for generative artificial intelligence, requiring conversion of abstract literary prose into long-form, multi-scene visual narratives. While current video generation models excel at short, single-scene clips within narrow temporal and spatial contexts, novel-to-film generation operates in a more complex regime, demanding long-duration content across diverse scenes with dynamically evolving entity states. To address this, we formalize novel-to-film generation as dynamic cinematic world modeling, decomposed into two phases: construction, which grounds abstract, underspecified literary narratives into concrete, stateful, and persistent world entities; and evolution, which governs how these entities dynamically update under plot progression to maintain causal consistency across scenes. We propose FilmWorld, an end-to-end agentic system where two groups of specialized agents collaborate to instantiate these phases. Construction-side agents perform narrative structured translation, world entity state modeling with visual anchoring, and state-driven shot planning, progressively projecting literary language into a cinematic blueprint. Evolution-side agents perform state-anchored visual generation, cross-shot dynamic state propagation, and closed-loop state verification to maintain causal consistency and visual coherence. To address the evaluation gap in long-form generation, we introduce FilmEval, a systematic evaluation framework that couples a difficulty-graded benchmark of 15 representative novels with an automated protocol of nine objective metrics spanning three dimensions: cinematic presentation, film consistency, and novel fidelity. Experiments demonstrate that FilmWorld consistently outperforms state-of-the-art video generation agent systems, with particularly pronounced improvements in narrative fidelity and cross-scene consistency.

</details>


### [56] [Gaze-DETR: Top-Down Guidance Through Priority Maps for Infrared Weak-Small UAV Detection with DETR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19040)
*Nian Liu,Yuxin Yang,Shubo Lin,Sikui Zhang,Liang Li,Boyu Cai,Yizheng Wang,Weiming Hu,Jin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Gaze-DETR，一种受生物视觉启发的红外小目标检测器，通过在学习目标定位前先学习内部优先级图（priority map），结合三种监督方式（边界框高斯图、真实注视图、迁移伪注视图），在TIR-UAV120-Gaze和Anti-UAV410数据集上取得了优异性能，证明了显式空间优先级学习能提供定位前的互补引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标检测中，传统方法依赖边界框监督来指定目标位置，但缺乏在定位前对候选区域进行优先级排序或保留弱目标证据的显式引导。受任务驱动视觉搜索机制启发，本文旨在通过先学习空间优先级图来提供定位前的引导信息，从而提升检测性能。

Method: 提出Gaze-DETR框架，包含三个模块：(1) Priority Head：从图像特征预测归一化的优先级图；(2) Residual Priority-Guided Feature Modulation (RPFM)：增强高优先级响应同时保留多尺度特征；(3) Priority-Guided Anchor Query Injection (PAQI)：将高优先级位置转换为解码器锚点查询。优先级头训练采用三种监督方案：边界框导出的高斯图、真实注视图（基于注视密度图）、以及从注视-边界框关系学习的迁移伪注视图。

Result: 在TIR-UAV120-Gaze上，边界框监督获得85.76 mAP$_{50}$和88.77 F1，真实注视监督获得86.18 mAP$_{50}$和89.00 F1。在Anti-UAV410上，边界框监督获得87.06 mAP$_{50}$和90.90 F1，迁移伪注视监督获得87.08 mAP$_{50}$和90.43 F1。

Conclusion: 显式空间优先级学习能够在不同标注设置和成本下提供与边界框监督互补的定位前引导，有效提升红外小目标检测性能。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection (ISTD) remains challenging because tiny, low-contrast targets are easily overwhelmed by clutter, noise, or occlusion. Conventional single-frame and multi-frame detectors rely on bounding-box supervision, which specifies final target locations but offers little explicit guidance for prioritizing candidate regions or preserving weak-target evidence before localization. Task-driven visual search offers such guidance: top-down goals and visual evidence jointly form a spatial priority map that ranks candidate locations. Building on this principle, we propose Gaze-DETR, a bio-inspired detector that learns an internal priority map before localization. First, a priority head predicts a normalized priority map from image features. Second, Residual Priority-Guided Feature Modulation (RPFM) enhances high-priority responses while retaining multi-scale features. Finally, Priority-Guided Anchor Query Injection (PAQI) converts high-priority locations into decoder anchor queries. We train the priority head using three supervision schemes: box-derived Gaussian maps; real-gaze maps constructed from fixation-density maps; and transferred pseudo-gaze maps learned from gaze--box relations in paired annotations and applied to Anti-UAV410 training boxes. To support the latter two schemes, we construct TIR-UAV120-Gaze with paired detection and task-driven eye-tracking annotations. On TIR-UAV120-Gaze, Gaze-DETR achieves 85.76 mAP$_{50}$ and 88.77 F1 with box-derived supervision, and 86.18 mAP$_{50}$ and 89.00 F1 with real-gaze supervision. On Anti-UAV410, it achieves 87.06 mAP$_{50}$ and 90.90 F1 with box-derived supervision, and 87.08 mAP$_{50}$ and 90.43 F1 with transferred pseudo-gaze supervision. These results show that explicit spatial-priority learning provides pre-localization guidance complementary to bounding-box supervision across annotation settings and costs.

</details>


### [57] [Contrastive On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19046)
*Jiacheng Ruan,Jun Tang,Wenzhen Yuan,Ting Liu,Shuai Bai,Dayiheng Liu,Zhibo Yang,Yuzhuo Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出COPD框架，通过对比指令下的教师模型评分差异作为token级优势信号，指导学生模型学习更简洁高效的推理策略，在9个多模态基准上显著减少推理长度且不损失性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有On-policy Distillation方法依赖token级分布匹配，缺乏显式信号来比较不同推理模式下token的相对兼容性，无法直接建模模式间的偏好。

Method: 对学生模型生成的每个token，用冻结的教师模型在两种对比指令（轻推理与重推理）下评分，将log概率差值作为token级优势信号指导OPD更新。

Result: 在9个多模态基准上，COPD显著减少推理长度而不影响性能，在不同任务和模型规模上均提升效率。对比形式可无缝集成到OPSD框架中实现自蒸馏。

Conclusion: COPD通过对比性监督直接鼓励学生模型学习更简洁高效的推理策略，超越了传统单一教师分布模仿的局限。

Abstract: On-policy Distillation (OPD) supervises a student model on trajectories sampled from its own policy by minimizing the divergence between the output distributions of the teacher and student at each token position, thereby providing dense token-level supervision. Although existing OPD methods have demonstrated strong performance in improving the reasoning ability of student models, their objectives fundamentally rely on token-level distribution matching. Consequently, they lack an explicit signal for comparing a token's relative compatibility across reasoning modes and thus do not directly model preferences between these modes. To address this limitation, we propose COPD, a contrastive OPD framework. Specifically, for each token generated by the student model, a frozen teacher model scores the same student state under two contrasting instructions that elicit light and heavy reasoning. The difference between the resulting log probabilities serves as a token-level advantage signal to guide the OPD update. Rather than merely imitating a single teacher distribution, COPD directly encourages the student model to learn more concise and efficient reasoning strategies. We conduct experiments on nine multimodal benchmarks covering both reasoning and understanding tasks. The results show that COPD substantially reduces reasoning length without compromising model performance and consistently improves efficiency across different tasks and model scales. Furthermore, the contrastive formulation can be seamlessly integrated into the On-policy Self-distillation (OPSD) framework, where self-contrastive supervision is constructed without an additional teacher model, thereby enabling the model to distill itself toward lightweight reasoning.

</details>


### [58] [Now You See the Hate: Adaptive View Retrieval for Hidden Hateful Illusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19061)
*Qianpu Chen,Derya Soydaner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应视图检索（Adaptive View Retrieval）框架，通过检索和校准机制检测仇恨性光学幻觉中的隐藏有害信息，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有方法，达到93.2%的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态安全系统在检测仇恨性光学幻觉方面存在严重不足，传统分类器和视觉语言模型准确率极低（≤24.5%），大量隐藏仇恨内容无法被识别。

Method: 将隐藏仇恨幻觉检测形式化为感知检索问题，提出自适应视图检索框架：构建互补视图库和隐藏信息模板，自适应选择可信视图，检索隐藏信息身份，并校准恢复的证据是否具有危害性。

Result: 在HatefulIllusion上达到93.2%平衡准确率，显著优于原始视图基线；在多个基准测试中超越微调CLIP基线，匹配或超越人类表现。

Conclusion: 稳健的多模态审核需要在决定内容是否有害之前先恢复其隐藏含义。

Abstract: Hateful optical illusions expose a serious gap in current multimodal safety systems. On original-view hateful illusions, previous work shows that six moderation classifiers achieve at most 20.9 to 24.5% accuracy and nine state-of-the-art VLMs remain at or below 10.2% with illusion-aware prompting, leaving most hidden hate undetected. We formulate hidden hateful illusion detection as a perceptual retrieval problem and propose Adaptive View Retrieval. This retrieve-and-calibrate framework assembles a complementary view bank for the image and hidden-message templates, adaptively selects which views to trust, retrieves hidden-message identities, and calibrates whether the recovered evidence is harmful. On HatefulIllusion with a frozen CLIP encoder, Adaptive View Retrieval reaches 93.2% balanced accuracy on the held-out test split. It substantially outperforms original-view baselines and fixed single-transform filters across hate slangs, hate symbols, and visibility levels. The same design also surpasses official fine-tuned CLIP baselines, matches or exceeds human performance on IllusionMNIST, IllusionFashionMNIST, and IllusionAnimals, and outperforms zoom-out preprocessing on HC-Bench under the SemVink protocol. Together, these results show that robust multimodal moderation requires recovering hidden meaning before deciding whether it is harmful.

</details>


### [59] [Delineate Anything v2: A Global Foundation Model for Field Delineation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19069)
*Mykola Lavreniuk,Nataliia Kussul,Andrii Shelestov,Yevhenii Salii,Volodymyr Kuzin,Charlotte Julia Li-Xing Wang,Zoltan Szantoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Delineate Anything v2，一个面向全球大尺度农业地块边界分割的基础模型。通过构建包含6100万实例的多分辨率数据集FBIS-73M，并设计分辨率特定的数据清洗流程解决地块合并问题，模型在100个国家零样本评估中相比SOTA提升103.3% mAP@0.5，并在消费级工作站上5.4小时完成乌克兰全国60.3万平方公里制图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉基础模型（如SAM）在遥感农业地块边界分割中因拓扑复杂性、纹理模式及缺乏物理尺度感知而表现不佳，且缺乏面向全球大尺度的高质量数据集与评估基准。

Method: 1）构建FBIS-73M多分辨率数据集（6100万实例，61个国家）；2）提出分辨率特定的数据清洗流程，通过拓扑图像空间自适应同质化合并地块并增强弱物理边界；3）建立涵盖100个国家的人工标注零样本评估基准。

Result: Delineate Anything v2 超越当前SOTA（包括Delineate Anything），mAP@0.5提升0.284（相对增益103.3%），在消费级工作站上5.4小时完成乌克兰全国60.3万平方公里制图。

Conclusion: 该模型实现了快速的国家乃至全球尺度部署能力，代码、权重、数据集及矢量产品均已开源。

Abstract: Accurate agricultural field boundary delineation at large scale is a foundational task for food security, supply chain transparency, and carbon accounting. While vision foundation models like SAM show remarkable zero-shot capabilities, they frequently fail in geospatial domains due to topological complexity, cropland texturing patterns, and a lack of physical scale awareness. In this work, we introduce Delineate Anything v2, a globally scalable foundation model designed specifically for wide-area field boundary mapping. We construct FBIS-73M, a 73-million-instance multi-resolution dataset spanning 61 countries. To address the pervasive issue of multi-field administrative parcel merging, we introduce a resolution-specific data curation pipeline that leverages topological image-space adaptation to homogenize merged parcels and strengthen weak physical boundaries. Furthermore, we establish a novel, manually curated evaluation benchmark covering 100 countries to assess independent zero-shot generalization. Our results show that Delineate Anything v2 surpasses the current state-of-the-art, including the Delineate Anything framework, by 0.284 mAP@0.5 (+103.3% relative gain), while maintaining execution speeds suitable for rapid national- and global-scale deployment, as demonstrated by nationwide mapping of Ukraine (603,000 km^2) in 5.4 hours on a consumer-grade workstation. Code, pre-trained weights, the FBIS-73M dataset, and ready-to-use national-scale vector boundary products are publicly available at https://github.com/Lavreniuk/Delineate-Anything.

</details>


### [60] [Context-structured Video Anomaly Detection with Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19077)
*Dongjun Kim,Changjae Oh,Andrea Cavallaro,Jeonghoon Mo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CSI-VAD是一种无需训练的视频异常检测方法，通过将视频分解为环境、对象和时间三个上下文进行分支推理，在多个基准上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测方法依赖大量标注数据，而近期的大规模视觉-语言模型虽支持无训练推理，但大多采用整体式推理，可能遗漏上下文特定的异常线索。

Method: 将视频分解为环境、对象和时间三种不同上下文，分别进行上下文特定的分支推理，无需预定义文本提示或数据集特定调优。

Result: 在UCF-Crime和UBnormal数据集上，CSI-VAD一致优于直接整体式基线方法，并与现有方法相比具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 结构化的上下文分解策略对于无需训练的视频异常检测具有显著优势，能够更准确地捕获不同场景下的异常线索。

Abstract: Training video anomaly detectors is challenging due to the difficulty and cost of annotating diverse and rare abnormal events. Although recent large vision-language models enable training-free inference, existing approaches mostly rely on holistic inference over sampled video and may miss context-specific anomaly cues. In this paper, we present CSI-VAD, a training-free video anomaly detector that identifies abnormal events across diverse contexts. The key idea is to decompose each video into three distinct contexts (environment, objects, time) and perform context-specific inference in separate branches. Because we ground anomaly judgments solely in context-specific visual cues, we do not require predefined text prompts describing abnormal events or dataset-specific tuning. Experiments on UCF-Crime and UBnormal show that CSI-VAD consistently improves over the direct holistic baseline and achieves competitive performance against existing methods, showing the advantage of structured context decomposition for training-free video anomaly detection.

</details>


### [61] [Advancing Multimodal Fusion on Heterogeneous Medical Data with Hybrid Geometry Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19086)
*Joy Dhar,Manish Kumar Pandey,Nayyar Zaidi,Chen Chen,Maryam Haghighat,Ferdous Sohel,Puneet Goyal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CURE框架，通过轻量级HyFuse层逐步融合多种医疗模态数据，在16个公开数据集上提升性能达3.97%，降低计算成本达87.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态融合方法存在三大挑战：难以有效捕获跨模态交互、计算成本高、且仅针对固定模态组合设计，缺乏对异构医疗模态的适应性和泛化性。

Method: 提出CURE框架，包含高效混合几何感知融合层（HyFuse），其内部采用残差卷积模块提取多尺度特征，混合空间感知注意力机制学习由粗到细的结构线索，并通过互补的可学习后期融合与共享信息精炼模块学习模态顺序不变的表征。

Result: 在16个公开数据集上，CURE性能提升最高达3.97%，计算成本降低高达87.8%，优于主流多模态融合方法。

Conclusion: CURE框架通过渐进式模态融合和轻量化设计，有效解决了多模态医疗数据融合中的性能与效率问题，具有良好的适应性和泛化性。

Abstract: Multimodal fusion learning (MFL) has shown great potential in the medical domain, where we are faced with disparate data modalities such as imaging, clinical records, and omics. However, existing MFL strategies face several major challenges. First, they struggle to capture complex cross-modal interactions effectively, which in turn limits performance improvements. Second, they incur high computational costs, restricting their applicability in resource-constrained healthcare AI applications. Finally, they are often designed and evaluated for narrow, fixed modality configurations (e.g., imaging-only, or specific pairs such as image and omics), which limits evidence of their adaptability and generalizability to broader collections of heterogeneous medical modalities. To address these challenges, we propose a novel MFL framework - Cascaded Unified Representation Learning for Efficient Fusion Network (CURE) - a lightweight and scalable framework that progressively integrates various modalities through a novel efficient Hybrid Geometry Aware Fusion layer (HyFuse), where each HyFuse layer is sequentially learned for each modality, making the framework adaptable and generalizable. Within HyFuse, an efficient residual convolution module captures rich multi-scale features to ensure cost-effective learning, while a hybrid-space aware attention mixer learns coarse-to-fine structural cues to better preserve cross-modal relationships. Complementary learnable late-fusion and shared information refinement modules are then employed to learn robust modality-order-invariant shared representations, which in turn yields consistent performance improvements. Extensive evaluations on 16 public datasets show that CURE outperforms leading multimodal fusion methods, boosting performance by up to 3.97% and lowering computational costs by up to 87.8%, ensuring more effective and reliable predictions.

</details>


### [62] [FlexiAvatar: Unified 3D Gaussian Human Avatars Under Arbitrary Body Visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19100)
*Yihalem Yimolal Tiruneh,Muhammad Salman Ali,Uyoung Jeong,Muneeb A. Khan,MD Khalequzzaman Chowdhury Sayem,Allanur Bayramgeldiyev,Binod Bhattarai,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexiAvatar是一个从单目视频重建可动画化3D人体虚拟形象的统一框架，仅优化可见身体区域，利用遮挡鲁棒的SMPL-X跟踪、部位特定残差细化和扩散模型补全不可见区域，在多个数据集上平均PSNR提升约3%，并降低了计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将所有身体区域（包括不可见的）联合优化，导致可见区域 fidelity 下降并产生伪影。FlexiAvatar旨在解决这一问题，通过仅优化可见区域来提升重建质量。

Method: 1) 遮挡鲁棒的SMPL-X跟踪；2) 部位特定残差细化以捕捉高频几何和外观细节；3) 扩散模型生成与观测外观一致的不可见区域纹理；4) 仅对可见身体区域进行显式优化。

Result: 在NeuMan、ZJU-MoCap、WildAvatar（全身）、talk-show（上半身/半身）和INSTA（仅头部）数据集上均优于SOTA方法，平均PSNR提升约3%，同时减少了计算和内存开销。

Conclusion: FlexiAvatar通过仅优化可见区域有效消除了不可见肢体带来的伪影，结合扩散模型补全实现了高质量完整人体重建，在多种人体部位输入场景下均表现优异。

Abstract: Reconstructing animatable 3D human avatars from monocular video is a fundamental problem in computer vision with broad applications in AR/VR and digital content creation. Existing approaches typically couple parametric body models with neural rendering or 3D Gaussian splatting and optimize all body regions jointly from short videos, which often degrades fidelity in the visible areas. To overcome this limitation, we introduce FlexiAvatar, a unified framework that explicitly optimizes only the visible body regions, effectively eliminating artifacts arising from unobserved limbs. Our method integrates occlusion-robust SMPL-X tracking with part-specific residual refinement to capture high-frequency geometric and appearance details. To complete entirely unseen regions (e.g., back views), we leverage a diffusion-based approach to generate texture consistent with the observed appearance. Experiments on full-body (NeuMan, ZJU-MoCap, WildAvatar), upper/half-body (talk-show clips), and head-only (INSTA) inputs show that FlexiAvatar delivers consistently higher reconstruction quality, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by an average PSNR improvement of approximately 3% across datasets. Finally, by restricting optimization to observed regions, our method reduces the effective number of Gaussians that must be optimized and rendered, leading to reduced runtime and memory overhead in partial-visibility scenarios.

</details>


### [63] [GATE-3D: Geometry-Aware Test-time Adaptive Reranking for Open-Set 3D Shape Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19111)
*Hao Wu,Heyi Lin,Zilin Wang,Huizai Yao,Hao Wang,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GATE-3D是轻量级查询自适应重排序方法，通过选择性融合几何与外观特征提升3D形状检索性能，在三个开放集基准上超越纯外观检索和始终融合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外观预训练模型会混淆外观相似但几何不同的3D形状，而简单融合几何与外观特征会在两种模态已对齐时损害检索性能。

Method: 提出GATE-3D，一个轻量级查询自适应重排序方法。对每个查询，利用跨模态不一致性特征预测几何感知分数应多大程度调整外观排序，实现选择性几何融合，无需重新训练检索骨干网络。

Result: 主基准上mAP@10提升2.00点(p=0.041)；几何假阳性减少10.8%；在低数据场景下线性路由优于小型MLP。

Conclusion: 跨模态不一致性特征比模型容量对自适应路由更重要，选择性融合比始终融合更鲁棒。

Abstract: Large pretrained vision models have substantially improved appearance-based 3D shape retrieval, but they still confuse shapes that look similar while differing in geometry. Although geometry-aware features can reduce these errors, naive fusion of geometry and appearance may hurt retrieval when the two modalities are already well aligned. We propose GATE-3D, a lightweight query-adaptive reranking method that incorporates geometry without retraining the retrieval backbone. For each query, GATE-3D predicts how much a geometry-aware score should adjust the appearance-based ranking using features that capture disagreement between the two modalities. This selective design lets geometry contribute where it helps and stay silent where it would hurt. Experiments on three open-set 3D retrieval benchmarks show that GATE-3D improves over appearance-only retrieval and is more robust than always-on fusion. On the primary benchmark, it improves mAP@10 by 2.00 points over appearance-only retrieval (p=0.041); it also improves leave-one-category-out generalization and reduces geometric false positives by 10.8%. GATE-3D achieves competitive zero-shot results against DAC-based baselines. We further find that simple linear routing is more effective than a small MLP in the low-data regime, suggesting that cross-modal disagreement features matter more than model capacity for adaptive routing.

</details>


### [64] [CR-Refiner: An Object-Centric Optimal Transport Reranker for Edit-Conditioned 3D Scene Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19115)
*Hao Wu,Jinjing Zhu,Nanyu Wu,Qianyi Cai,Heyi Lin,Hao Wang,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CR-Refiner，一种无需训练的重新排序方法，用于编辑条件3D场景检索。该方法结合冻结大语言模型解析编辑查询、不平衡最优传输匹配单实体查询与候选场景、轴条件结构先验和LLM验证器，并在新发布的3D-CER基准上显著超越了三个基线检索器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三类方法（2D图像检索、3D基础编码器、3D场景定位）均无法有效完成编辑条件3D场景检索任务，且缺乏相应的评估基准。

Method: CR-Refiner包含三个组件：1) 冻结LLM将编辑解析为结构查询；2) 基于不平衡最优传输的单-多匹配器，通过1xG成本矩阵耦合类别、风格、材质和几何信息，允许单实体查询丢弃无关物体质量；3) 轴条件结构先验（大小关键词和方向线索）和LLM验证器（对前三候选进行连续置信度细化）。

Result: 在三个定性不同的基线检索器上，CR-Refiner在3D-CER基准的每个编辑轴上均一致提升了硬子集的R@1和mAP@10。

Conclusion: CR-Refiner作为无需训练的重新排序器，能有效提升编辑条件3D场景检索性能，同时新发布的3D-CER基准填补了该领域评估空白。

Abstract: Edit-conditioned 3D scene retrieval pairs a reference 3D room with a natural-language modification and retrieves rooms from a corpus that satisfy the edit. Three lines of prior work each fall short on this task. 2D composed image retrieval reasons over pixel-level edits and has no primitive for 3D object sets. 3D foundation encoders embed individual objects but cannot compose at the scene level. 3D scene-grounding methods localize references inside a static scene rather than rank modified rooms across a corpus. We present CR-Refiner, a training-free reranker that wraps any base retriever's top-K candidates with three components. A frozen LLM parses the edit into a structured query entity, and each candidate is scored by an unbalanced optimal-transport problem over a 1xG cost matrix coupling category, style, material, and geometry. The unbalanced solver lets the single-entity query drop mass on irrelevant objects, modelling the asymmetry directly. An axis-conditional structural prior adds size-keyword cues for geometric edits and subject-anchor direction cues for spatial edits. An LLM verifier refines the top three candidates with continuous confidence. Because no benchmark evaluates compositional matching over 3D object sets, we additionally release 3D-CER, 4,963 edit-conditioned queries over a 23,381-room indoor corpus across five edit axes, with multi-positive ground truth, CIRR-style hard subsets, and zero-target adversarials. Across three qualitatively distinct base retrievers, CR-Refiner consistently improves hard-subset R@1 and mAP@10 on every edit axis.

</details>


### [65] [Latent Riemannian Flow Matching for Geometry-Grounded 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19120)
*Lisa Weijler,Irene Ballester,Guofeng Mei,Tolga Birdal,Pedro Hermosilla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为几何引导黎曼流匹配（Geometry-Grounded Riemannian Flow Matching）的方法，通过在VGGT几何基础模型的潜在空间中进行流匹配，实现了从无位姿图像生成连贯3D场景的新范式。该方法在黎曼流形（四超球面乘积空间）上操作，避免了标准欧氏流匹配的失效问题，在多个3D场景基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何基础模型（如VGGT）虽能提供强3D先验，但仅能进行前馈确定性预测，无法生成输入视图未直接支持的几何内容。而3D场景生成模型需要强几何先验来从稀疏输入产生连贯输出。本文旨在弥合这两种范式之间的鸿沟。

Method: 在VGGT的潜在空间（由四个超球面组成的乘积流形）上执行黎曼流匹配（Riemannian Flow Matching），确保生成的tokens保持在冻结解码头所需的有效数据流形上，并与VGGT的多尺度编码器对齐。

Result: 在RealEstate10K、ScanNet++和ETH3D数据集上，该方法在逐视图外观和聚合3D几何质量上均优于最近的场景生成基线方法。

Conclusion: 在几何基础模型的潜在空间上进行流匹配是3D生成的一种可行且有效的新范式。

Abstract: Geometric foundation models, such as the Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT), provide strong 3D priors from unposed images. However, such models operate purely in a feed-forward, deterministic regime, \ie~they cannot generate plausible geometry beyond what the input views directly support. Generative models for 3D scenes, on the other hand, must rely on strong geometric priors to produce coherent outputs from sparse inputs. We bridge these two paradigms by performing flow matching directly in VGGT's latent space, leveraging its learned 3D priors without committing to any explicit downstream representation such as Gaussians, meshes, or video-VAE latents. This requires respecting the latent geometry: VGGT tokens occupy a product of high-dimensional hyperspheres on which standard Euclidean flow matching fails. We address this with a Riemannian Flow Matching framework defined on a product manifold of four hyperspheres, aligned with VGGT's multi-scale encoder, which keeps generated tokens on the valid data manifold required by the frozen decoding heads. On RealEstate10K, ScanNet++ and ETH3D, our method achieves strong performance against recent scene generation baselines in both per-view appearance and aggregated 3D geometry, establishing latent-space flow matching on geometric foundation models as a viable paradigm for 3D generation. The project page can be found $\href{https://lisaweijler.github.io/geometry-grounded-rfm/}{\text{here}}$.

</details>


### [66] [Text Template Tokens Are Implicit Semantic Registers in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19139)
*Maohua Li,Qirui Li,Yanke Zhou,Yiduo Li,Zhaosheng Chi,Chao Xu,Cuifeng Shen,Yixuan Xu,Hanlin Tang,Kan Liu,Tao Lan,Lin Qu,Shao-Qun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过因果可解释性框架揭示了文本到图像扩散Transformer（DiT）中结构模板令牌虽不含提示信息，却意外成为图像到文本的注意力汇聚点并维持目标身份，进而提出了一种无需训练的头剪枝规则（可减少20%注意力FLOPs，GenEval仅降1.4分），并展示了DiT中生成计算如何按头与深度组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对文本到图像扩散Transformer（DiTs）在去噪过程中的内部计算理解不足，尤其是在结构令牌与提示令牌的角色和相互作用方面缺乏因果层面的解释。

Method: 提出一种因果可解释性框架，结合注意力分解与跨令牌跨度、头、层的定向干预，分离提示内容令牌与结构模板令牌，并分析其信息流与因果作用。

Result: 结构模板令牌在编码器输出时几乎不含提示信息，但成为图像到文本的注意力汇聚点，因果上维持着目标身份；提示语义先注入图像潜在表示，再被模板令牌读取；对提示令牌注意力最强的头是可剪枝的，可减少20%注意力FLOPs，GenEval仅下降1.4分。

Conclusion: 输入时编码语义的令牌不一定是在生成过程中维持语义的令牌；论文提供了DiTs内部机制的因果视角，揭示了语义路由与视觉合成的分离以及从身份形成到传播与精化的组织层次。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion transformers (DiTs) jointly process text and image tokens, yet their internal computation during denoising remains poorly understood. We introduce a causal interpretability framework for modern large-scale DiTs that combines attention decomposition with targeted interventions across token spans, heads, and layers. Using it to separate prompt-content tokens from structural template tokens, we find that the structural tokens carry little prompt-specific information at the encoder output. Yet surprisingly, they emerge as dominant image-to-text attention sinks and causally maintain object identity inside the DiT, acting as implicit semantic registers. We show that they acquire this identity indirectly, with prompt semantics first injected into the image latents and then read back into the template tokens rather than transferred directly from the prompt tokens. Inspired by the above findings, we design a training-free pruning rule for DiTs. Heads that attend most strongly to prompt tokens are dispensable, and pruning them removes $20\%$ of attention FLOPs with only a $1.4$-point drop on GenEval. We further reveal how generative computation in DiTs is organized across heads and depth, separating semantic routing from visual synthesis and progressing from identity formation to propagation and refinement. Our work not only reveals that the tokens encoding semantics at input need not be those that maintain it during generation, but also provides a causal view of internal mechanisms in DiTs.

</details>


### [67] [Point Ladder Tuning: Parameter-Efficient Hierarchical Adaptation for 3D Point Cloud Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19171)
*Junlin Chang,Longhao Zou,Rui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Point Ladder Tuning (PLT)，一种面向点云骨干网络的局部感知参数高效微调框架。它通过分层梯形网络、局部-全局融合和动态提示生成器三个模块，在冻结骨干网络的情况下实现层次化、实例自适应的多尺度局部特征注入，仅需极少可训练参数即可在分类和密集预测任务上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有点云骨干网络通过激进的token化和下采样获得紧凑的全局token，但不可逆地丢失了细粒度局部几何信息，导致现有PEFT方法仅能在粗化token上调节全局语义，难以恢复丢失的多尺度局部特征。

Method: PLT包含三个组件：(i) 分层梯形网络(HLN)从原始点云构建多分辨率局部特征金字塔；(ii) 局部-全局融合(LGF)对齐并融合局部金字塔与骨干网络中间语义；(iii) 动态提示生成器生成实例感知的多尺度提示来调节冻结的骨干网络。对于密集预测，还引入轻量级分割头逐步上采样融合特征并利用骨干先验细化细粒度结构。

Result: 分类任务仅用2.71%可训练参数，密集预测用7.69%可训练参数即达到SOTA性能；在PointGPT-L大骨干上仅需0.36%参数。

Conclusion: PLT通过层次化局部感知适配，有效弥补了现有PEFT方法在点云上丢失多尺度局部信息的缺陷，是一种高效且可扩展的点云参数微调范式。

Abstract: Fine-tuning pre-trained point-cloud backbones typically updates all parameters, resulting in substantial computation and memory overhead. More importantly, modern point backbones rely on aggressive tokenization and downsampling, which yields compact global tokens but irreversibly discards fine-grained local geometry, an inherent bottleneck for parameter-efficient adaptation. Consequently, existing PEFT methods that operate only on these coarsened tokens can modulate global semantics but struggle to recover the missing multi-scale locality. We present Point Ladder Tuning (PLT), a locality-aware PEFT framework that performs hierarchical, instance-conditioned adaptation while keeping the backbone frozen. PLT forms a lightweight closed loop: (i) a Hierarchical Ladder Network (HLN) constructs a multi-resolution local feature pyramid directly from raw points; (ii) a Local-Global Fusion (LGF) aligns and fuses local pyramids with intermediate backbone semantics; and (iii) a Dynamic Prompt Generator produces instance-aware multi-scale prompts to modulate the frozen backbone effectively. For dense prediction, we further introduce a lightweight segmentation head that progressively upsamples fused features and leverages backbone priors to refine fine structures. Extensive experiments on classification and dense prediction show that PLT consistently surpasses prior PEFT baselines with minimal tunable parameters. PLT achieves state-of-the-art performance using only 2.71% trainable parameters for classification and 7.69% for dense prediction, and scales favorably to larger backbones, requiring merely 0.36% parameters on PointGPT-L. The code is released at https://github.com/JunLinChang/ECCV2026-PLT.

</details>


### [68] [ABot-World-0: Infinite Interactive World Rollout on a Single Desktop GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19191)
*Fan Jiang,Zhaoxu Sun,Mengchao Wang,Ziyu Zhu,Chiyu Wang,Yunpeng Zhang,Wenlin Liu,Yun Wang,Xue Zheng,Rui Sun,Junfeng Ni,Hongyu Pan,Zhongxu Sun,Fei Yu,Zengye Ge,Mengmeng Du,Nianfei Fan,Mingchao Sun,Yu Liu,Yongchang,Yanqing Zhu,Jiahang Wang,Ning Ying,Yuze Xuan,Di Yang,Zhicheng Liu,Zhe Gao,Tingbing Xu,Jiacheng Sui,Wenjin Yang,Junnan Lai,Shufeng Liu,Yuan Liu,Zheng Zhou,Yingliang Peng,Dawei Cao,Kaifeng Sheng,Yuxiang Cai,Fei Lu,Mu Xu,Ning Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ABot-World-0是一个基于动作条件的视频世界模型，支持实时、长程闭环交互。通过多源数据（AAA游戏、模拟引擎、网络视频）学习可控世界动态，采用WorldExplorer进行智能体驱动的数据收集，经过14项确定性质量检查、VLM评估和同步标注。通过教师强制和ODE蒸馏将双向动作条件教师模型逐步蒸馏为因果学生模型，并引入LongForcing对齐长程学生自 rollout 与扩展视野教师。支持原始键盘动作控制场景漫游和第三人称角色交互，使用参考角色记忆保持身份一致性。通过流式推理栈（轻量VAE解码器、高效注意力、内存感知调度、低比特DiT推理），在单张RTX 5090上以16 FPS实时生成720P视频，延迟1.2秒，峰值显存约19GiB。实验表明其具有强可控性和连贯的长程世界演化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在长程交互中面临分布偏移和自回归漂移问题，且缺少支持实时、闭环交互的高效推理方案。本文旨在构建一个既能实时生成高质量视频，又能支持长程、可控交互的统一世界模型。

Method: 多源数据基础设施（AAA游戏、模拟引擎、网络视频）+ WorldExplorer智能体驱动数据收集+14项质量检查+VLM评估+动作和文本标注；双向动作条件教师模型通过教师强制和ODE蒸馏为学生模型；LongForcing对齐长程自 rollout；原始键盘动作统一控制接口；参考角色记忆保持身份一致性；流式推理栈（轻量VAE解码器、高效注意力、内存感知调度、低比特DiT推理）

Result: 在单张RTX 5090上以16 FPS实时生成720P视频，动作到首帧延迟1.2秒，峰值显存约19GiB；在WorldRoamBench和扩展交互 rollout 中展现出强可控性和连贯的长程世界演化能力。

Conclusion: ABot-World-0成功实现了实时、长程闭环交互的视频世界模型，证明了多源数据训练、蒸馏策略和高效推理栈的有效性，为可控视频生成和世界模型研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present ABot-World-0, an action-conditioned video world model for real-time, long-horizon closed-loop interaction, supported by a multi-source data infrastructure spanning AAA games, simulation engines, and internet videos to learn controllable world dynamics. WorldExplorer performs agent-driven collection guided by training feedback, while a unified pipeline applies 14 deterministic quality checks, VLM-based assessment, and synchronized action and text annotation. We progressively distill a bidirectional action-conditioned teacher into a causal student through teacher forcing and ODE distillation, and introduce LongForcing to align long student self-rollouts with an extended-horizon teacher, mitigating accumulated distribution shift and autoregressive drift. Raw keyboard actions provide a unified control interface for scene roaming and third-person character interaction, while reference-character memory provides persistent appearance cues for identity consistency during third-person rollouts. For deployment, we co-design a streaming inference stack with a lightweight VAE decoder, efficient attention, memory-aware scheduling, and low-bit DiT inference. Across optimized low-bit configurations, ABot-World-0 streams 720P video at up to 16 FPS on a single NVIDIA RTX 5090 desktop GPU, with 1.2s action-to-first-frame latency and approximately 19GiB peak VRAM. Experiments on WorldRoamBench and extended interactive rollouts demonstrate competitive controllability and coherent long-horizon world evolution.

</details>


### [69] [Anatomy-Aware 3D Mesh Refinement of Pericardium Segmentations on Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19210)
*Andreas W. Aspe,Jonas Jalili Loft,Michael Huy Cuong Pham,Andreas Ohrt Johansen,Jørgen Tobias Kühl,Klaus Fuglsang Kofoed,Kristine Aavild Sørensen,Rasmus R. Paulsen,Josefine Vilsbøll Sundgaard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种3D迭代网格细化框架，利用解剖上下文和几何力来优化心包分割，在多个数据集上显著提升了分割精度，尤其对弱分割模型效果更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心包在CT图像中对比度低，难以准确分割，而准确分割心包对量化心外膜脂肪组织至关重要。

Method: 提出3D迭代网格细化框架，利用3D向量场迭代推动网格顶点至正确解剖位置，平衡解剖和几何力，作为模型无关的后处理步骤。

Result: 在高质量内部数据集和粗糙公开数据集上，所有体积、表面和解剖指标均一致提升，对弱分割模型提升更显著。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为通用后处理框架，有效提升心包分割质量，尤其适用于域外模型和训练数据有限的场景，且可扩展至其他解剖结构。

Abstract: Accurate delineation of the pericardium in a cardiac CT scan is essential for quantifying epicardial adipose tissue, yet it remains one of the most challenging structures to segment due to its poor contrast boundaries. Instead of solely relying on image gradients, our framework leverages the anatomical context of surrounding anatomical structures to guide the segmentation. This work introduces a novel 3D iterative mesh refinement framework that balances anatomical and geometric forces derived from inherent anatomical rules to refine an initial, possibly ambiguous, segmentation into a high-precision, anatomically plausible result. Designed as a model-agnostic post-processing step, our method uses a 3D vector field to iteratively push the vertices to the correct anatomical locations. Evaluating the refinement on both a high-resolution in-house dataset and a coarse, sparsely annotated open-source dataset, our method consistently improves all volumetric, surface, and anatomical metrics. The framework demonstrates greater improvement when applied to weaker initial segmentations, highlighting its potential for improving segmentations for out-of-domain models and in limited-training-data scenarios. The method is formulated as a gradient-based, GPU-accelerated framework that can be easily extended to other anatomical use cases.

</details>


### [70] [IGGT4D: Streaming 4D Instance-Grounded Geometry Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19228)
*Zhengyu Zou,Hao Li,Kuixuan Jiao,Liu Liu,Tingyang Xiao,Xiaolin Zhou,Fangzhou Hong,Zhizhong Su,Dingwen Zhang,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IGGT4D，一种流式实例引导的几何Transformer，用于在线4D场景理解。它通过因果时空建模顺序处理视频帧，增量更新相机运动、几何和物体身份的联合表示，实现长序列动态场景下的几何-实例一致性前馈重建。同时构建了InsScene4D-147K大规模数据集，在3D重建、姿态估计、实例空间跟踪和开放词汇分割任务上超越现有流式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式3D重建方法多侧重于几何，缺乏时序一致的物体级理解；语义重建和3D视觉-语言方法依赖外部2D语义线索或松散耦合的几何输入，难以在长动态场景中实现统一的几何-实例联合学习。

Method: 提出IGGT4D流式实例引导几何Transformer，采用因果时空建模逐帧处理视频，复用历史上下文，增量更新统一的相机运动、几何和物体身份表示。同时构建InsScene4D-147K数据集，通过自动化几何引导标注流水线生成时序一致的实例掩码。

Result: 在3D重建、姿态估计、实例空间跟踪和开放词汇分割任务上，IGGT4D均优于现有流式基线方法，并支持长动态序列的可扩展在线推理。

Conclusion: IGGT4D通过流式实例引导的几何Transformer有效解决了动态场景中几何与实例的联合学习问题，结合大规模高质量数据集，为在线4D场景理解提供了统一且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world spatial intelligence requires agents to understand scenes from continuous video streams, where objects move, persist, disappear, and reappear over time. While recent spatial foundation models have enabled generalizable feed-forward 3D reconstruction, most streaming methods remain geometry-centric and lack temporally consistent object-level understanding. Meanwhile, existing semantic reconstruction and 3D-aware vision-language methods largely rely on externally extracted 2D semantic cues or loosely coupled geometry inputs, limiting unified geometry-instance learning in long dynamic scenes. In this paper, we propose IGGT4D, a streaming instance-grounded geometry Transformer for online 4D scene understanding. IGGT4D processes video frames sequentially, reuses historical context through causal spatial-temporal modeling, and incrementally updates a unified representation of camera motion, geometry, and object identity. This enables long-sequence feed-forward reconstruction with geometry-instance consistency in dynamic environments. To address the lack of high-quality 4D supervision, we further construct InsScene4D-147K, a large-scale dataset spanning real/synthetic and static/dynamic scenes, with RGB images, depth, poses, and temporally consistent instance masks generated by an automated geometry-guided annotation pipeline. Experiments on 3D reconstruction, pose estimation, instance spatial tracking, and open-vocabulary segmentation demonstrate that IGGT4D outperforms existing streaming baselines while maintaining scalable online inference for long dynamic sequences.

</details>


### [71] [PathAgentBench: Benchmarking Evidence-Seeking Vision-Language Models on Whole-Slide Pathology Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19261)
*Dankai Liao,Tianyi Zhang,Yufeng Wu,Xinyue Zhang,Qiaochu Xue,Zeyu Liu,Dachun Zhao,Linghan Cai,Yueming Jin*

Main category: cs.CV
